u^{\varepsilon,\delta}
by the Aubin-Lions lemma.

Lemma 5.11
.

There hold

\left\{\begin{aligned} u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\rightarrow u\quad&\text{strongly in}~L^{2}(0,T;L^{q}(\Omega_{x}))~\text{for}~q\in\big[1,6\big)\\
u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\rightarrow u\quad&\text{strongly in}~L^{\tilde{q}}((0,T)\times\Omega_{x})~\text{for}~\tilde{q}\in\big[1,\frac{10}{3}\big)\end{aligned}\right.

(5.66)

as
\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

Proof.

Note that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}u^{\varepsilon,\delta}

\displaystyle=-(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\delta}+\Delta u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\nabla_{x}P^{\varepsilon,\delta}+\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(v-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dv\right)

\displaystyle=\sum^{4}_{i=1}J_{i}.

For
J_{1}
, by Hölder’s inequality, (
3.27
) and (
3.34
), we have

\displaystyle-\int_{(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}}(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\cdot\chi dxds

\displaystyle=\int_{(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}}(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot\nabla_{x}\chi\cdot u^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxds

\displaystyle\leq\int^{T}_{0}\|\nabla_{x}\chi\|_{L^{2}_{x}(\Omega_{x})}\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|^{2}_{L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x})}ds

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla_{x}\chi\|_{L^{4}(0,T;L^{2}_{x}(\Omega_{x}))}\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|^{2}_{L^{\frac{8}{3}}(0,T;L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}))}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla_{x}\chi\|_{L^{4}(0,T;L^{2}_{x}(\Omega_{x}))}\left(\|\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x})}+\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}))}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla_{x}\chi\|_{L^{4}(0,T;L^{2}(\Omega_{x}))},

where
\chi\in C_{c}^{\infty}((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x})\cap L^{4}(0,T;H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}))
,
\mbox{\rm supp\,}\chi\subset\Omega_{x}
and
\nabla\cdot\chi=0
.

For
J_{2}
, applying integration by parts, followed by Hölder’s inequality, and then using the uniform bound (
3.27
), we obtain:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega_{x}}\Delta u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\chi dxds

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega_{x}}\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\delta}:\nabla_{x}\chi dxds

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla_{x}\chi\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\Omega_{x}))}\|\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\Omega_{x}))}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla_{x}\chi\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\Omega_{x}))}.

For
J_{3}
, it follows from integration by parts that

\displaystyle-\int_{(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}}\nabla_{x}P^{\varepsilon,\delta}\cdot\chi dxds=\int_{(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}}P^{\varepsilon,\delta}\nabla_{x}\cdot\chi dxds=0.

For
J_{4}
, by (
3.27
), the embedding inequality (
3.34
) and (
5.49
), this yields

\displaystyle\int_{(0,T)\times R^{3}_{v}\times\Omega_{x}}

\displaystyle(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast\chi)\cdot(v-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dvdxds

\displaystyle\leq\int_{(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast\chi|dvdxds+\int_{(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast\chi|dvdxds

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}))}\|\chi\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}))}\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}_{v}^{3}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dv\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{\frac{3}{2}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}))}

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt+C\|\chi\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}))}\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}_{v}^{3}}|v|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dv\right\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{\frac{6}{5}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}))}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\chi\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}))}\leq C\|\chi\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{1}(\Omega_{x})}.

Thus we get

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{\frac{4}{3}}(0,T;H^{-1}(\Omega_{x}))}\leq C,

along with

\displaystyle\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{1}(\Omega_{x}))}\leq C

and the Aubin-Lions lemma yields

\displaystyle u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\rightarrow u\quad\text{strongly in}~L^{2}((0,T)\times\Omega_{x}).

Using Hölder’s inequality and (
3.34
), the proof of (
5.66
) is complete.

MM

\square

5.5.
The strong convergence of
f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}
in
L^{p}((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})

Lemma 5.12
.

Assume that
(u^{\varepsilon,\delta},f^{\varepsilon,\delta})
is a weak solution to the NSVFP equation (
1
), there holds

\left\{\begin{aligned} \left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{p}_{txv}}&\leq C_{1}(T+1)e^{6T}&&\text{as}~p\in(1,+\infty),\\
\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{1}_{txv}}&\leq C_{2}(T+1)^{5}e^{12T}&&\text{as}~p=1\end{aligned}\right.

and

\left\{\begin{aligned} \left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta})^{2}\right\|_{L^{p}_{txv}}&\leq C_{1}(T+1)e^{6T}&&\text{as}~p\in(1,+\infty),\\
\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta})^{2}\right\|_{L^{1}_{txv}}&\leq C_{2}(T+1)^{5}e^{12T}&&\text{as}~p=1,\end{aligned}\right.

(5.67)

where

\left\{\begin{aligned} &C_{1}=C(\left\|f_{in}\right\|_{L^{1}_{xv}},\left\|f_{in}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{xv}}),\\
&C_{2}=C\left(\|f_{in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{xv}},\|f_{in}\|_{L^{1}_{xv}},\left\||v|^{2}f_{in}\right\|_{L^{1}_{xv}},\|u_{in}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}},\|f_{in}\log f_{in}\|_{L^{1}_{xv}}\right).\end{aligned}\right.

Proof.

Setting
Q=(0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
, we have

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\int_{Q}|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{p}dxdvds

\displaystyle=\int_{Q\cap\{{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}}>e\}}|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{p}dxdvds+\int_{Q\cap\{{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}}\leq e\}}|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{p}dxdvds

(5.68)

\displaystyle=L_{1}+L_{2}

for any
p\in(1,+\infty)
.

For
L_{1}
, the inequality

\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\quad\text{for}\quad f^{\varepsilon,\delta}>e

leads to the following
L^{p}
-estimate:

\displaystyle\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}>e\}}|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{p}dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}>e\}}|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{2p}dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|^{2p}_{L^{2p}(Q)}.

For
L_{2}
, due to
\left|(f^{\varepsilon,\delta})^{1-\frac{1}{p}}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right|\leq\frac{p}{(p-1)e}
, it shows that

\displaystyle\int_{Q\cap\{{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}}\leq e\}}|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{p}dxdvds\leq C\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|^{p}_{L^{1}((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{z})}.

To sum up, we derive

\displaystyle\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|^{p}_{L^{p}\big((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\big)}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|^{2p}_{L^{2p}\big((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\big)}+\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|^{p}_{L^{1}\big((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\big)}\right).

By (
2.22
) and interpolation inequality for
L^{p}
-norms, it follows

\displaystyle\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{p}\big((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\big)}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|^{2p}_{L^{2p}\big((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\big)}+\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|^{p}_{L^{1}\big((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\big)}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\leq C(\left\|f_{in}\right\|_{L^{1}_{xv}},\left\|f_{in}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{xv}})(T+1)e^{6T},

for any
p\in(1,+\infty)
.
Thus we have

\displaystyle\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{txv}}\leq C(\left\|f_{in}\right\|_{L^{1}_{xv}},\left\|f_{in}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{xv}})(T+1)e^{6T}.

For
p=1
, the estimates (
3.31
) and (
4.43
) yield

\displaystyle\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{1}_{txv}}\leq C_{2}(T+1)^{5}e^{12T},

where
C_{2}=C\left(\|f_{in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{xv}},\|f_{in}\|_{L^{1}_{xv}},\left\||v|^{2}f_{in}\right\|_{L^{1}_{xv}},\|u_{in}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}},\|f_{in}\log f_{in}\|_{L^{1}_{xv}}\right).
Similarly, (
5.67
) also can be obtained.

MM

\square

Lemma 5.13
.

f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\to f\log f
converges strongly in
L^{p}((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})
for
p\in(1,+\infty)
.

Proof.

We divide the following inequality into four parts as follows. The difference of logarithmic terms admits the decomposition:

\displaystyle\int_{Q}

\displaystyle|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\log f|^{p}\,dx\,dv\,ds

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<1\}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}>f\}\end{subarray}}|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\log f|^{p}\,dx\,dv\,ds

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<1\}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<f\}\end{subarray}}|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\log f|^{p}\,dx\,dv\,ds

\displaystyle\quad+C\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}>1\}}|(f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f)\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{p}\,dx\,dv\,ds

\displaystyle\quad+C\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}>1\}}|f(\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\log f)|^{p}\,dx\,dv\,ds

\displaystyle:=K_{1}+K_{2}+K_{3}+K_{4}.

For
K_{1}
, it shows that

\displaystyle K_{1}=

\displaystyle\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<1\}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}>f\}}\left|(f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f)\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}+f\log\frac{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}}{f}\right|^{p}dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<1\}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}>f\}}|(f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f)\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{p}dxdvds

\displaystyle+C\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<1\}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}>f\}}\left|f\log\frac{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}}{f}\right|^{p}dxdvds

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle K_{11}+K_{12}.

For
K_{11}
, as the proof of
L_{2}
in (
5.5
), this yields

\displaystyle K_{11}

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<1\}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}>f\}}|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f|dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq C\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\|_{L^{1}((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}.

For
K_{12}
, by the inequality
\log(1+\tilde{f})\leq\tilde{f}
when
\tilde{f}>-1
, we obtain

\displaystyle K_{12}=

\displaystyle\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<1\}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}>f\}}|f\log(1+\frac{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f}{f})|^{p}dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<1\}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}>f\}}|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f|^{p}dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\|^{p}_{L^{p}((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}.

For
K_{2}
, it follows that

\displaystyle K_{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<1\}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<f\}}\left|(f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta})\log f+f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log\frac{f}{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}}\right|^{p}dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<1\}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<f\}}|(f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta})\log f|^{p}dxdvds

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+C\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<1\}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<f\}}\left|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log\frac{f}{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}}\right|^{p}dxdvds

\displaystyle=C\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<1\}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<f\}\cap\{f<1\}}|(f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta})\log f|^{p}dxdvds

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+C\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<1\}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<f\}\cap\{f\geq 1\}}|(f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta})\log f|^{p}dxdvds

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+C\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<1\}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<f\}}\left|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log\frac{f}{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}}\right|^{p}dxdvds

\displaystyle:=K_{21}+K_{22}+K_{23}.

For
K_{21}
, as the proof of
L_{2}
in (
5.5
), it becomes

\displaystyle K_{21}

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<1\}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<f\}\cap\{f<1\}}|f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{p(1-\kappa)}dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq C\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\|^{p(1-\kappa)}_{L^{p(1-\kappa)}((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}.

For
K_{22}
, we get

\displaystyle K_{22}

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<1\}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<f\}\cap\{f\geq 1\}}|(f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f)f|^{p}dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq C\|f\|^{p}_{L^{\infty}((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\|^{p}_{L^{p}((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}.

For
K_{23}
, as the proof in
K_{12}
, there holds

\displaystyle K_{23}

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<1\}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<f\}}|f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{p}dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq C\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\|^{p}_{L^{p}((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}.

For
K_{3}
, the following estimates hold:

\displaystyle K_{3}

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}>1\}}|(f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f)f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{p}dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq C\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|^{p}_{L^{\infty}((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\|^{p}_{L^{p}((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}.

For
K_{4}
, we have

\displaystyle K_{4}

\displaystyle=\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}>1\}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}>f\}}\left|f\log\left(1+\frac{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f}{f}\right)\right|^{p}\,dx\,dv\,ds

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}>1\}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<f\}}\left|f\log\left(1+\frac{f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta}}{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}}\right)\right|^{p}\,dx\,dv\,ds

\displaystyle\leq\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}>1\}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}>f\}}|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f|^{p}\,dx\,dv\,ds+\int_{Q\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}>1\}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}<f\}}\left|f\left(\frac{f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta}}{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}}\right)\right|^{p}\,dx\,dv\,ds

\displaystyle\leq C\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\|^{p}_{L^{p}(Q)}.

To sum up, by Lemma
5.8
, this lemma is proven.

MM

\square

Lemma 5.14
.

Let
\Omega_{x}\subset\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}
be a bounded domain. The following uniform bound holds:

\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta})^{2}\|_{L^{1}((0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}\leq C,

where
C
is independent of
\varepsilon
and
\delta
.

Proof.

By (
2.17
), it shows that

\displaystyle\int_{(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}

\displaystyle f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta})^{2}dvdxds

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\frac{4}{3}\pi\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta})^{2}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{txv}}\right)\int_{(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta})^{2}dv\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}dxds.

For
\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta})^{2}dv\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}
, due to

\displaystyle\left|f^{\frac{1}{8}}\log f\right|\leq\frac{8}{e},

(5.69)

we find

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\int_{(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta})^{2}dv\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}dxds

\displaystyle=\int_{(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}>e\}}|v|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta})^{2}dv+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\cap\{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\leq e\}}|v|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta})^{2}dv\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}dxds

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v|(f^{\varepsilon,\delta})^{3}dv\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}dxds+C\int_{(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v|(f^{\varepsilon,\delta})^{\frac{3}{4}}dv\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}dxds

\displaystyle:=k_{1}+k_{2}.

For
k_{1}
, by Hölder’s inequality, it becomes that

\displaystyle k_{1}\leq C\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}^{\frac{3}{2}}\left\||v|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{1}((0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}^{\frac{3}{4}}.

For
k_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle k_{2}\leq\int_{(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\frac{1}{1+|v|}(|v|+1)^{2}(f^{\varepsilon,\delta})^{\frac{3}{4}}dv\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}dxds,

and by Hölder’s inequality about
v
, it becomes

\displaystyle\leq\int_{(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\frac{1}{(1+|v|)^{4}}dv\right)^{\frac{3}{16}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}(1+|v|)^{\frac{8}{3}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dv\right)^{\frac{9}{16}}dxds

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\int_{(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}(1+|v|)^{\frac{8}{3}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dvdxds\right)^{\frac{9}{16}}.

By (
2.22
) and (
3.29
), we derive

\displaystyle\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta})^{2}\right\|_{L^{1}((0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}\leq C.

MM

\square

Lemma 5.15
.

f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\rightarrow f\log f
strongly in
L^{1}((0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})
as
\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0
.

Proof.

Firstly, by Hölder’s inequality, it follows that

\displaystyle\int_{(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}}

\displaystyle\int_{|v|\geq R}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|dvdxds\leq

\displaystyle\left(\int_{(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}}\int_{|v|\geq R}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dvdxds\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}}\int_{|v|\geq R}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta})^{2}dvdxds\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Under the result of (
5.58
), we derive

\displaystyle\sup_{\varepsilon,\delta}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega_{x}}\int_{|v|\geq R}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|dvdxds\rightarrow 0,~\text{as}~R\rightarrow\infty.

(5.70)

Then, by Lemma
5.13
, one has

\displaystyle f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\rightarrow f\log f~\text{a.e. on}~(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}.

Finally, we prove
\int_{(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dvdxds
is equi-integrable. Due to (
5.70
), this yields the followings: For
\forall\varepsilon_{1}>0,\exists R>0
, while
|v|>R
, for
\forall\varepsilon,\delta
,
s.t.

\displaystyle\sup_{\varepsilon,\delta}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega_{x}}\int_{|v|\geq R}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\left|\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right|dvdxds<\varepsilon_{1}

(5.71)

holds.
For any
E\subset(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
, if
E\subset(0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times B_{R}
,

\mu(E)<\frac{\varepsilon_{1}}{\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{txv}}}
, then

\displaystyle\sup_{\varepsilon,\delta}\int_{E}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\left|\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right|dvdxds\leq\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{txv}}\mu(E)<\varepsilon_{1}

(5.72)

holds.
By the Lebesgue-Vitali theorem (Lemma
A.6
), we prove this lemma.

MM

\square

Lemma 5.16
.

Under the assumptions stated in Theorem
1.4
,
it holds

\displaystyle\left\||v|^{q}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{1}((0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}\leq C

(5.73)

with
q\in[0,\frac{\kappa}{2}]
. Moreover, the following strong convergence holds:

|v|^{p}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\to|v|^{p}f\log f\quad\text{strongly in }L^{1}((0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})

for all exponents
p\in[0,\frac{\kappa}{2})
.

Proof.

The first step is to establish the bound

\left\||v|^{q}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{1}((0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}\leq C.

Using Hölder’s inequality, it becomes

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\left\||v|^{q}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{1}((0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}

\displaystyle\leq\left\||v|^{2q}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{1}((0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta})^{2}\right\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{L^{1}((0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}.

By Lemma
3.7
and Lemma
5.14
, it follows that

\displaystyle\left\||v|^{p}(f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\log f)\right\|_{L^{1}_{txv}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\||v|^{\frac{\kappa}{2}}\left|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\log f\right|\right\|^{\frac{2p}{\kappa}}_{L^{1}_{txv}}\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\log f\right\|^{\frac{\kappa-2p}{\kappa}}_{L^{1}_{txv}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\log f\right\|^{\frac{\kappa-2p}{\kappa}}_{L^{1}_{txv}}.

By Lemma
5.15
, the lemma is proved.

MM

\square

6.
Verification of the conditions in
i)-iv)
of Definition
1.1
.

In this section, we will verify the conditions in
i)-iv)
in Definition
1.1
and show the global energy inequality that is satisfied for
\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0
. Firstly, let us review some necessary estimates in Section 3. By Proposition
3.1
, Lemma
3.2
, Lemma
3.3
, Proposition
3.4
and Lemma
3.8
, we have

\displaystyle\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t;L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}))}+\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}

(6.74)

\displaystyle+\|\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}((0,t)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}+\left\||v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t;{L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})})}+\left\||v|^{3}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t;L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}))}

\displaystyle+\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(0,t;L^{1}\left(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\right)\right)}+\|\nabla u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,t;{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x})})}+\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}(0,t;{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x})})}

\displaystyle+\left\|P^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\frac{5}{3}}((0,t)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}\leq C\left(t+1\right)^{3}\exp\left[C(t+1)^{3}e^{4t}\right],

where

\displaystyle C=C\left(\|f_{in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{xv}},\|f_{in}\|_{L^{1}_{xv}},\left\||v|^{3}f_{in}\right\|_{L^{1}_{xv}},\|u_{in}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}},\|f_{in}\log f_{in}\|_{L^{1}_{xv}},\left\||x|^{2}f_{in}\right\|_{L^{1}_{xv}}\right).

Furthermore, applying Tao’s method to the a priori estimates of the Navier-Stokes equations, the following estimate is obtained:

\displaystyle\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{1}(0,t;L^{\kappa+3}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}))}+\left\||v|^{\kappa}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t;L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}))}\leq C(t+1)^{\frac{3\kappa+12}{2}}e^{C(\kappa)(t+1)^{\frac{14}{5}}e^{4t}},

(6.75)

where
\kappa\in[3,+\infty)
and

\displaystyle C=C\left(\kappa,\|f_{in}\|_{L^{1}_{xv}},\|f_{in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{xv}},\left\||v|^{\kappa}f_{in}\right\|_{L^{1}_{xv}},\|u_{in}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\right),

as a consequence of Lemmas
3.6
and
3.7
.

Verification of a priori norms in
i)
of Definition
1.1
.

Due to Lemma
5.11
, (
6.74
) and weak lower semi-continuity, there hold

\displaystyle\|u\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}(0,t;{L^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})})}

\displaystyle\leq\liminf_{\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0}\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}(0,t;{L^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})})};

(6.76)

\displaystyle\|u\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,t;{\dot{H}^{1}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})})}

\displaystyle\leq\liminf_{\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0}\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(0,t;{\dot{H}^{1}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})})};

(6.77)

\displaystyle\left\|P\right\|_{L^{\frac{5}{3}}((0,t)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}

\displaystyle\leq\liminf_{\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0}\left\|P^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\frac{5}{3}}((0,t)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}

(see also the arguments by constructing an auxiliary function in the
t
direction, Theorem 4.2 in
[
5
]
).
Moreover, it follows from Theorem
1.4
and Lemma
5.8
that

f\geq 0
, which along with Fatou’s lemma in
xv
, weak* lower semi-continuity in
t
and Lemma
5.13
implies that

\displaystyle\left\|f\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(0,t;L^{1}\left(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\right)\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\liminf_{\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0}\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(0,t;L^{1}\left(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\right)\right)};

\displaystyle\left\|f\log f\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(0,t;L^{1}\left(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\right)\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\liminf_{\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0}\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(0,t;L^{1}\left(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\right)\right)}.

(6.78)

Due to (
3.30
) and (
6.74
), also by Fatou’s lemma in
xv
and weak* lower semi-continuity in
t
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\left(|x|^{2}+|v|^{2}\right)f\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(0,t;L^{1}\left(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\right)\right)}\leq\liminf_{\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0}\left\|\left(|x|^{2}+|v|^{2}\right)f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(0,t;L^{1}\left(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\right)\right)}.

(6.79)

For (
2.23
), it follows that

\displaystyle\|f\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{p}_{xv}((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{6})}\leq\liminf_{\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0}\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{p}_{xv}((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{6})}}\leq e^{\frac{3(p-1)}{p}T}\|f_{in}\|_{L^{p}_{xv}(\mathbb{R}^{6})}

for arbitrary
p\in[1,+\infty)
, which yields

\displaystyle\|f\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{6}_{xv})}\leq e^{3T}\|f_{in}\|_{L^{\infty}_{xv}(\mathbb{R}^{6})}.

Verification of a priori norms in
ii)
of Definition
1.1
.
From Lemma
3.6
, Lemma
3.7
, Lemma
5.11
and Lemma
5.16
, using Fatou’s lemma, we have

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L^{1}(0,t;L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}))}

\displaystyle\leq\liminf_{\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0}\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{1}(0,t;L^{p}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}))};

\displaystyle\left\||v|^{\kappa}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t;L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}))}

\displaystyle\leq\liminf_{\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0}\left\||v|^{\kappa}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t;L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}))};

\displaystyle\left\||v|^{\frac{\bar{\kappa}}{2}}f\log f\right\|_{L^{1}((0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}

\displaystyle\leq\liminf_{\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0}\left\||v|^{\frac{\bar{\kappa}}{2}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{1}((0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})},

where the second inequality uses weak* lower semi-continuity in
t
.
Based on the above inequalities, we derive

\displaystyle\left\||v|^{\bar{\kappa}}f\log f\right\|

{}_{L^{\infty}\left(0,t;L^{\eta}(\mathbb{R}_{x}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}_{v}^{3})\right)}

\displaystyle=\left\||v|^{\bar{\kappa}}f\log f\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(0,t;L^{\eta}(\mathbb{R}_{x}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}_{v}^{3})\right)\cap\{f>1\}}+\left\||v|^{\bar{\kappa}}f\log f\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(0,t;L^{\eta}(\mathbb{R}_{x}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}_{v}^{3})\right)\cap\{f\leq 1\}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\left\||v|^{\bar{\kappa}}f^{2}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(0,t;L^{\eta}(\mathbb{R}_{x}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}_{v}^{3})\right)\cap\{f>1\}}+\left\||v|^{\bar{\kappa}}f^{1-\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(0,t;L^{\eta}(\mathbb{R}_{x}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}_{v}^{3})\right)\cap\{f\leq 1\}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\sup_{t}\left(\left[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{6}_{xv}}|v|^{\eta\bar{\kappa}}f^{2\eta}dxdv\right]^{\frac{1}{\eta}}+\left[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{6}_{xv}}|v|^{\eta\bar{\kappa}}f^{(1-\varepsilon)\eta}dxdv\right]^{\frac{1}{\eta}}\right)

with
(1-\varepsilon)\eta=1
and
\eta\bar{\kappa}=\kappa
. Due to the bound of
\left\||v|^{\kappa}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t;L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}))}
and
\|f\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\mathbb{R}^{6}_{xv})}
, we get

\displaystyle\left\||v|^{\bar{\kappa}}f\log f\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(0,t;L^{\eta}(\mathbb{R}_{x}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}_{v}^{3})\right)}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\left(t+1\right)^{\frac{3\kappa+12}{2}}\exp\left[C(\kappa)(t+1)^{3}e^{4t}\right]\right)^{2}+C\left(\left(t+1\right)^{\frac{3\kappa+12}{2}}\exp\left[C(\kappa)(t+1)^{3}e^{4t}\right]\right)^{1-\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(t+1\right)^{3\kappa+12}\exp\left[C(\kappa)(t+1)^{3}e^{4t}\right].

Verification of weak solutions in
iii)
of Definition
1.1
.

For
(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})n^{\varepsilon,\delta}
, by Corollary
5.5
we get

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}n^{\varepsilon,\delta}(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot\Psi dxds\rightarrow\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}nu\cdot\Psi dxds

as
\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

For
(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\otimes u^{\varepsilon,\delta}
, by Lemma
5.11
we get

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\otimes(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}):\nabla\Psi dxds\rightarrow\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}u\otimes u:\nabla\Psi dxds

as
\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

Combining (
6.74
), the functions
u
and
f
solve the Navier-Stokes-Vlasov-Fokker-Planck equations (
1
) in the sense of distributions:

\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\nabla u:\nabla\Psi-u\otimes u:\nabla\Psi-u\cdot\partial_{t}\Psi\big)(s,x)dxds

=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}u_{in}(x)\Psi(0,x)dx+\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(v-u)fdv\Psi(s,x)dxds,

with

\nabla u:\nabla\Psi=\sum_{j,k=1}^{d}\partial_{j}u^{k}\partial_{j}\Psi^{k}\quad\text{and}\quad u\otimes u:\nabla\Psi=\sum_{j,k=1}^{d}u^{j}u^{k}\partial_{j}\Psi^{k}
.

\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}f(\partial_{t}\Phi+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\Phi+(u-v)\cdot\nabla_{v}\Phi+\Delta_{v}\Phi)dxdvdt=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}f_{in}\Phi(0,x,v)dxdv,

for any
\Psi\in C^{1}_{0}([0,T);(C_{c}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}))^{3})
with
\nabla\cdot\Psi=0
and

\Phi\in C^{1}_{0}([0,T);C_{c}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}))
.

Verification of global energy inequality in
iv)
of Definition
1.1
.

Next, we prove the convergence of global energy inequality. For the left-hand side of (
4.43
), by Fatou’s lemma in
xv
and weak* lower semi-continuity in
t
, it shows that

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}f\log fdxdv\leq

\displaystyle\liminf_{\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0}\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdv,

where it is similar with (
6.78
). Others are the same as in (
6.76
), (
6.77
) and (
6.79
).

Next we prove

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}|u(t,\cdot)|^{2}dx+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{6}_{xv}}\left(\frac{1}{2}|v|^{2}f+f\log f\right)(t,\cdot)dxdv+\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{x}^{3}}|\nabla u|^{2}dxds

\displaystyle+\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}\frac{|(u-v)f-\nabla_{v}f|^{2}}{f}dxdvds

(6.80)

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}^{6}_{xv}}\left(\frac{1}{2}|v|^{2}f_{in}+f_{in}\log f_{in}\right)dxdv+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}|u_{in}|^{2}dx.

For
\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}\frac{|(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-v)f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{2}}{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}}dxdvds
, it follows that

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}\frac{|(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-v)f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{2}}{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}}dxdvds

\displaystyle=\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds+4\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}\left|\nabla_{v}\sqrt{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}}\right|^{2}dxdvds

\displaystyle\hskip 170.71652pt-2\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-v)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds

\displaystyle=\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds+4\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}\left|\nabla_{v}\sqrt{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}}\right|^{2}dxdvds-6\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds

\displaystyle=\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds-2\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}v\cdot(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds+\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}|v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt+4\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}\left|\nabla_{v}\sqrt{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}}\right|^{2}dxdvds-6\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds

\displaystyle:=i_{1}+i_{2}+i_{3}+i_{4}+i_{5}.

For
i_{1}
, we derive

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}|u|^{2}f-|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds

\displaystyle=\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}|u|^{2}f-|u|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds+\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}|u|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds

\displaystyle:=i_{11}+i_{12}.

By (
2.17
), (
3.34
) and (
6.74
),
i_{11}
becomes

\displaystyle|i_{11}|

\displaystyle\leq C\left(1+\frac{4}{3}\pi\left\|f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{txv}}\right)\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}|u|^{2}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v|^{2}|f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|dv\right)^{\frac{3}{5}}dxds

\displaystyle\leq C\left(1+\frac{4}{3}\pi\left\|f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{txv}}\right)\int^{t}_{0}\left\|u\right\|_{L^{5}_{x}}^{2}\left\||v|^{2}(f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta})\right\|_{L^{1}_{xv}}^{\frac{3}{5}}ds

\displaystyle\leq C\left(1+\frac{4}{3}\pi\left\|f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{txv}}\right)\left\|u\right\|^{2}_{L^{\frac{20}{9}}_{t}L^{5}_{x}}\left\||v|^{2}(f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta})\right\|_{L^{6}_{t}L^{1}_{xv}}^{\frac{3}{5}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(1+\frac{4}{3}\pi\left\|f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{txv}}\right)\left(\left\|u\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\left\|u\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}\dot{H}^{1}_{x}}\right)^{2}\left\||v|^{2}(f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta})\right\|_{L^{6}_{t}L^{1}_{xv}}^{\frac{3}{5}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\||v|^{2}(f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta})\right\|_{L^{6}_{t}L^{1}_{xv}}^{\frac{3}{5}}.

Due to Corollary
5.10
, we have

i_{11}\rightarrow 0

as
\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.
For
i_{12}
, we derive

\displaystyle|i_{12}|

\displaystyle\leq\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}\left|(u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot(u+\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right|dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{v}^{3}\times\{|x|\leq R\}}\left|(u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot(u+\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right|dxdvds

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{v}^{3}\times\{|x|\geq R\}}\left|(u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot(u+\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right|dxdvds

\displaystyle:=i_{12-1}+i_{12-2}.

By (
2.17
), (
3.34
) and (
6.74
), it becomes

\displaystyle i_{12-1}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(1+\frac{4}{3}\pi\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{txv}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\int_{|x|\leq R}\left|(u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot(u+\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\right|\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dv\right)^{\frac{3}{5}}dxds

\displaystyle\leq Ct^{\frac{1}{20}}\left(1+\frac{4}{3}\pi\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{txv}}\right)\left\||v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{1}_{xv}}^{\frac{3}{5}}\left\|u+\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\frac{20}{9}}_{t}L^{5}_{x}}\left\|u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{5}(|x|\leq R))}

\displaystyle\leq C(t)\left\|u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{5}(|x|\leq R))},

and

\displaystyle i_{12-2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(1+\frac{4}{3}\pi\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{txv}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\int_{|x|\geq R}\left|(u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot(u+\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\right|\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dv\right)^{\frac{3}{5}}dxds

\displaystyle\leq Ct^{\frac{1}{10}}\left(1+\frac{4}{3}\pi\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{txv}}\right)\left\|u+\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\frac{20}{9}}_{t}L^{5}_{x}}\left\|u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\frac{20}{9}}_{t}L^{5}_{x}}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\left(\sup_{s\in[0,T]}\int_{\{|x|\geq R\}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdv\right)^{\frac{3}{5}}

\displaystyle\leq C(t)\left(\sup_{s\in[0,T]}\int_{\{|x|\geq R\}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|x|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|x|^{\frac{2}{3}}|v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdv\right)^{\frac{3}{5}}

\displaystyle\leq C(t)R^{-\frac{2}{5}}\left\||x|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{1}_{xv}}^{\frac{1}{5}}\left\||v|^{3}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{1}_{xv}}^{\frac{2}{5}}.

Due to Lemma
5.11
and the uniform boundedness property (
6.74
) and (
6.79
) with respect to
\varepsilon
and
\delta,
it is deduced that

i_{12}\rightarrow 0

as
\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0
.
To sum up, this leads to

\displaystyle\lim_{\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0}\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds=\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}|u|^{2}fdxdvds.

The limit result in
i_{2}
as
\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0
is the same as
i_{1}
. In fact,

\displaystyle|i_{2}|

\displaystyle\leq\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}\left|u\cdot vf-(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot vf^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right|dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}\left|u\cdot v(f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta})\right|dxdvds+\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}\left|(u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot vf^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right|dxdvds

\displaystyle:=i_{21}+i_{22}.

For
i_{21}
, by (
2.17
), (
3.34
) and (
6.74
), we have

\displaystyle|i_{21}|

\displaystyle\leq C\left(1+\frac{4}{3}\pi\left\|f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{txv}}\right)\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}|u|\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v|^{\frac{9}{5}}|f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|dv\right)^{\frac{5}{6}}dxds

\displaystyle\leq C\left(1+\frac{4}{3}\pi\left\|f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{txv}}\right)\int^{t}_{0}\left\|u\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\left\||v|^{\frac{9}{5}}(f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta})\right\|_{L^{1}_{xv}}^{\frac{5}{6}}ds

\displaystyle\leq C\left(1+\frac{4}{3}\pi\left\|f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{txv}}\right)\left\|u\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{6}_{x}}\left\||v|^{\frac{9}{5}}(f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta})\right\|_{L^{\frac{5}{3}}_{t}L^{1}_{xv}}^{\frac{5}{6}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(1+\frac{4}{3}\pi\left\|f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{txv}}\right)\left(\left\|u\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}_{x}}+\left\|u\right\|_{L^{2}_{t}\dot{H}^{1}_{x}}\right)\left\||v|^{\frac{9}{5}}(f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta})\right\|_{L^{\frac{5}{3}}_{t}L^{1}_{xv}}^{\frac{5}{6}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\||v|^{\frac{9}{5}}(f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta})\right\|_{L^{\frac{5}{3}}_{t}L^{1}_{xv}}^{\frac{5}{6}}.

Due to Corollary
5.10
, it is deduced that

i_{21}\rightarrow 0

as
\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

For
i_{22}
, we derive

\displaystyle|i_{22}|

\displaystyle\leq\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}\left|(u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot v)f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right|dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{v}^{3}\times\{|x|\leq R\}}\left|(u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot vf^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right|dxdvds

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{v}^{3}\times\{|x|\geq R\}}\left|(u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot vf^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right|dxdvds

\displaystyle:=i_{22-1}+i_{22-2}.

By (
2.17
), (
3.34
) and (
6.74
), it becomes

\displaystyle i_{22-1}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(1+\frac{4}{3}\pi\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{txv}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\int_{|x|\leq R}\left|u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right|\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dv\right)^{\frac{4}{5}}dxds

\displaystyle\leq Ct^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(1+\frac{4}{3}\pi\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{txv}}\right)\left\||v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{1}_{xv}}^{\frac{4}{5}}\left\|u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{5}(|x|\leq R))}

\displaystyle\leq C(t)\left\|u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{5}(|x|\leq R))},

and

\displaystyle i_{22-2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(1+\frac{4}{3}\pi\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{txv}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\int_{|x|\geq R}\left|u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right|\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dv\right)^{\frac{4}{5}}dxds

\displaystyle\leq Ct^{\frac{11}{20}}\left(1+\frac{4}{3}\pi\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{txv}}\right)\left\|u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\frac{20}{9}}_{t}L^{5}_{x}}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,T]}\int_{\{|x|\geq R\}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdv\right)^{\frac{4}{5}}

\displaystyle\leq C(t)\left(\sup_{s\in[0,T]}\int_{\{|x|\geq R\}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|x|^{-\frac{2}{3}}|x|^{\frac{2}{3}}|v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdv\right)^{\frac{4}{5}}

\displaystyle\leq C(t)R^{-\frac{8}{15}}\left\||x|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{1}_{xv}}^{\frac{4}{15}}\left\||v|^{3}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{1}_{xv}}^{\frac{8}{15}}.

Due to Lemma
5.11
and the uniform smallness property of
i_{22-2}
at infinity with respect to
\varepsilon
and
\delta,
it is deduced that

i_{22}\rightarrow 0

as
\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0
.
To sum up, this leads to

\displaystyle\lim_{\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0}\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}^{6}_{xv}}v\cdot(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds=\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}^{6}_{xv}}v\cdot ufdxdvds.

For
i_{3}
, it follows from Fatou’s lemma that

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}|v|^{2}fdxdvds

\displaystyle\leq\liminf_{\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0}\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}|v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds.

For
i_{4}
, it follows from the convexity of the entropy that

\displaystyle 4\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}\left|\nabla_{v}\sqrt{f}\,\right|^{2}dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq\liminf_{\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0}4\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}\left|\nabla_{v}\sqrt{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}}\,\right|^{2}dxdvds.

For
i_{5}
, due to the strong convergence of
f^{\varepsilon,\delta}
in
L^{1}((0,t)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})
, we have

\displaystyle-6\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}fdxdvds\rightarrow-6\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds,

as
\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0
.
Consequently, the following inequality holds:

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}\frac{\left|(u-v)f-\nabla_{v}f\right|^{2}}{f}dxdvds

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\leq\liminf_{\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0}\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}\frac{\left|(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-v)f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right|^{2}}{f^{\varepsilon,\delta}}dxdvds.

For the right-hand side of (
4.43
), by (
3.29
), Lemma
5.9
and the dominated convergence theorem we have

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}|v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(1-\gamma_{\delta}(v))dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}|v|^{2}|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f|(1-\gamma_{\delta}(v))dxdvds+\int_{(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}_{xv}^{6}}|v|^{2}f(1-\gamma_{\delta}(v))dxdvds

(6.81)

\displaystyle\rightarrow 0~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

For
f_{in}^{\delta}\log f_{in}^{\delta}
, due to (
1.12
), it follows that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{6}_{xv}}f_{in}^{\delta}\log f_{in}^{\delta}dxdv\rightarrow\int_{\mathbb{R}^{6}_{xv}}f_{in}\log f_{in}dxdv,~\text{as}~\delta\rightarrow 0.

To sum up, when
\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0
, the global energy inequality (
6
) is obtained.

7.
Verification of
v)
in Definition
1.1
: the convergence of local energy inequality of the first type

Verification of local energy inequality in
v)
of Definition
1.1
.
In the following, we will verify the local energy inequality in
v)
of Definition
1.1
. For the left-hand side of (
4.45
), the proof follows from the same procedure as in Section 6, and is therefore omitted. For the right-hand side of (
4.45
), the weak convergence of
P^{\varepsilon,\delta}
is first required. By the equation of
u^{\varepsilon,\delta}
, we have

\displaystyle-\Delta(P^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\bar{P}^{\varepsilon,\delta})=\partial_{j}(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}_{i})\partial_{i}u^{\varepsilon,\delta}_{j}+\nabla\cdot\left[\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}(v-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dv\right].

Using the same methods as Lemma
3.8
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}\left|P^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\bar{P}^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right|^{\frac{5}{3}}dxdt\leq C,

which implies

\displaystyle P^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\bar{P}^{\varepsilon,\delta}\rightharpoonup P-\bar{P}\quad\text{in}~L^{\frac{3}{2}}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

(7.82)

Next, we prove the convergence of the right-hand terms of the local energy inequality (
4.45
). Let us write the right terms of the local energy inequality (
4.45
) as
M_{1},M_{2},\cdots,M_{11}
term by term.

The estimate of
M_{1}
:
Since
\psi\in C^{\infty}_{c}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})
, we have
(\partial_{t}\psi+\Delta_{x}\psi)\in C^{\infty}_{c}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})
. Therefore, due to Lemma
5.11
, it shows that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{2}(\partial_{t}\psi+\Delta_{x}\psi)dxds\rightarrow\frac{1}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}|u|^{2}(\partial_{t}\psi+\Delta_{x}\psi)dxds,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

The estimate of
M_{2}
:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{2}(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi dxds-\frac{1}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}|u|^{2}u\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi dxds

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}(|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{2}-|u|^{2})(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi dxds+\frac{1}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}|u|^{2}(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-u)\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi dxds

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\|\nabla_{x}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}+u\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{4}(\Omega_{x}))}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t;L^{2}(\Omega_{x}))}\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-u\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{4}(\Omega_{x}))}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\frac{1}{2}\|\nabla_{x}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}\|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-u\|_{L^{3}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}\|u\|^{2}_{L^{3}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\|\nabla_{x}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}+u\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{4}(\Omega_{x}))}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t;L^{2}(\Omega_{x}))}\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-u\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{4}(\Omega_{x}))}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\frac{1}{2}\|\nabla_{x}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}\left(\|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u\|_{L^{3}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}+\|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u-u\|_{L^{3}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}\right)\|u\|^{2}_{L^{3}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}.

By Lemma
5.11
, it follows that

\displaystyle M_{2}\to\frac{1}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}|u|^{2}u\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi dxds~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

The estimate of
M_{3}
:
Since
\Omega_{v}
is a bounded domain, for any
v
, there exists a ball
B(0,R)
such that
v\in B(0,R)
. Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left[\left(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right)f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\left(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f\right)f\right](\partial_{t}\phi+\Delta_{v}\phi)dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq\frac{R^{2}}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left|(f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f)(\partial_{t}\phi+\Delta_{v}\phi)\right|dxdvds

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left(f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\log f\right)(\partial_{t}\phi+\Delta_{v}\phi)dxdvds.

By Lemma
5.8
and Lemma
5.13
, this yields

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right)f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(\partial_{t}\phi+\Delta_{v}\phi)dxdvds

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\to\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f\right)f(\partial_{t}\phi+\Delta_{v}\phi)dxdvds,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

The estimate of
M_{4}
:

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right)f^{\varepsilon,\delta}v\cdot\left(\nabla_{x}\phi-\nabla_{v}\phi\right)dxdvds

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt-\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f\right)fv\cdot\left(\nabla_{x}\phi-\nabla_{v}\phi\right)dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq\frac{R^{3}}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left|(f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f)\left(\nabla_{x}\phi-\nabla_{v}\phi\right)\right|dxdvds

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt+R\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left|(f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\log f)\left(\nabla_{x}\phi-\nabla_{v}\phi\right)\right|dxdvds

Since Lemma
5.8
and Lemma
5.13
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right)

\displaystyle f^{\varepsilon,\delta}v\cdot\left(\nabla_{x}\phi-\nabla_{v}\phi\right)dxdvds\rightarrow

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f\right)fv\cdot\left(\nabla_{x}\phi-\nabla_{v}\phi\right)dxdvds,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

The estimate of
M_{5}
:

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}(P^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\bar{P}^{\varepsilon,\delta})u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi dxds-\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}(P-\bar{P})u\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi dxds

\displaystyle=\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}(P^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\bar{P}^{\varepsilon,\delta})(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-u)\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi dxds+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}[(P^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\bar{P}^{\varepsilon,\delta})-(P-\bar{P})]u\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi dxds

\displaystyle\leq\|P^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\bar{P}^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-u\|_{L^{3}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}\|\nabla_{x}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}\left[(P^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\bar{P}^{\varepsilon,\delta})-(P-\bar{P})\right]u\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi dxds.

From (
5.66
), it follows that

\displaystyle\|P^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\bar{P}^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-u\|_{L^{3}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}\|\nabla_{x}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}\to 0,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

Since
|u\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi|\leq C|u||\nabla_{x}\psi|\in L^{3}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})
, by the definition of weak convergence and (
7.82
), it holds that

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}\left[(P^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\bar{P}^{\varepsilon,\delta})-(P-\bar{P})\right]u\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi dxds\to 0,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

The estimate of
M_{6}
:

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}fv\cdot u\phi dxdvds-\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}v\cdot(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\phi dxdvds

\displaystyle=\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}(f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta})v\cdot u\phi dxdvds+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}v\cdot(u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\phi dxdvds

\displaystyle:=M_{61}+M_{62}.

For the estimate of
M_{61}
, it holds that

\displaystyle M_{61}

\displaystyle\leq R\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}|(f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta})u|dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq CR\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}\|u\|_{L^{3}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}\left\|\int_{\Omega_{v}}(f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta})dv\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}

\displaystyle\leq C\|u\|_{L^{3}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}\|f-f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}

\displaystyle\leq Ct^{\frac{1}{12}}\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}\left((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}\right)}.

By Lemma
5.8
, it is concluded that

\displaystyle M_{61}\to 0,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

For the estimate of
M_{62}
, we have

\displaystyle M_{62}

\displaystyle\leq\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}|v|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}|dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq C\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}\left(\int_{\Omega_{v}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dv\right)\times|u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}|dxds

\displaystyle\leq C\|\phi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}\left\|\int_{\Omega_{v}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dv\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}\times\|u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{3}((0,t)\times(\Omega_{x}))}

\displaystyle\leq C\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}\|u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{3}((0,t)\times(\Omega_{x}))}

\displaystyle\leq C\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}\left(\|u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u\|_{L^{3}((0,t)\times(\Omega_{x}))}+\|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{3}((0,t)\times(\Omega_{x}))}\right).

By Lemma
5.11
, it implies that

\displaystyle M_{62}\to 0,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

In summary, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}v\cdot u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\phi dxdvds\to\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}fv\cdot u\phi dxdvds,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

The estimate of
M_{7}
:

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}v\cdot\left(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)\right)dxdvds-\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}fv\cdot u\psi dxdvds

\displaystyle=\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}j^{\varepsilon,\delta}u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi-ju\psi dxds+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}v\cdot(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)-u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)dvdxds

\displaystyle=M_{7-1}+M_{7-2}.

For
M_{7-1}
, Since (
5.51
), we derive

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}v\cdot u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi dxdvds\rightarrow\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}fv\cdot u\psi dxdvds,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

For
M_{7-2}
, it shows that

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}v\cdot(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)-u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)dvdxds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)-u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi||v|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{4}{3}\pi\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}}+1\right)\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)-u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi|\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dv\right)^{\frac{4}{5}}dxds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ct^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{4}{3}\pi\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}}+1\right)\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{5}(\Omega_{x}))}\left\||v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t;L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{6}_{xv}))}^{\frac{4}{5}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(t)\bigg(\|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u\psi)-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{5}(\Omega_{x}))}+\|u\psi-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u\psi)\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{5}(\Omega_{x}))}

\displaystyle+\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi-u\psi)\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{5}(\Omega_{x}))}\bigg)\rightarrow 0,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

To sum up, we have

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}v\cdot\left(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)\right)dxdvds\rightarrow\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}fv\cdot u\psi dxdvds,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

The estimate of
M_{8}
:

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left(2+\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right)f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot\nabla_{v}\phi dxdvds

\displaystyle\hskip 142.26378pt-\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left(2+\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f\right)fu\cdot\nabla_{v}\phi dxdvds

\displaystyle=\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left(2+\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}\right)f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\left(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-u\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}\phi dxdvds

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left(2+\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}\right)\left(f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\right)u\cdot\nabla_{v}\phi dxdvds

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left(f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\log f\right)(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot\nabla_{v}\phi dxdvds

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}f\log f\left(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-u\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}\phi dxdvds

\displaystyle:=M_{81}+M_{82}+M_{83}+M_{84}.

For the estimate of
M_{81}
, by Hölder’s inequality, (
5.49
) and the boundedness of the velocity
v
, we obtain

\displaystyle M_{81}

\displaystyle=\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left(2+\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}\right)f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\left(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-u\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}\phi dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq\left(2+\frac{R^{2}}{2}\right)\|\nabla_{v}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-u|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq Ct^{\frac{2}{3}}\left(2+\frac{R^{2}}{2}\right)\|\nabla_{v}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}\|n^{\varepsilon,\delta}(t,x)\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t;L^{\frac{3}{2}}(\Omega_{x}))}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\times\|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-u\|_{L^{3}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}

\displaystyle\leq Ct^{\frac{2}{3}}\left(2+\frac{R^{2}}{2}\right)\|\nabla_{v}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}\|n^{\varepsilon,\delta}(t,x)\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t;L^{\frac{3}{2}}(\Omega_{x}))}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\times\left(\|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u\|_{L^{3}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}+\|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u-u\|_{L^{3}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}\right).

By Lemma
5.11
, it follows that

\displaystyle M_{81}\to 0,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

For the estimate of
M_{82}
, applying Hölder’s inequality and the boundedness of the velocity
v
, we have

\displaystyle M_{82}

\displaystyle\leq\left(2+\frac{R^{2}}{2}\right)\|\nabla_{v}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}|(f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f)u|dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq C\left(2+\frac{R^{2}}{2}\right)\|\nabla_{v}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}\|u\|_{L^{3}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\times\left\|\int_{\Omega_{v}}|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f|dv\right\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(2+\frac{R^{2}}{2}\right)\|\nabla_{v}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}\|u\|_{L^{3}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\times\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}.

By Lemma
5.8
, it follows that

\displaystyle M_{82}\to 0,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

For the estimate of
M_{83}
, using Hölder’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle M_{83}

\displaystyle\leq\|\nabla_{v}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}\|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{2}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\log f\|_{L^{1}_{v}L^{2}_{xt}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla_{v}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{2}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\log f\|_{L^{2}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}.

By Lemma
5.13
, it follows that

\displaystyle M_{83}\to 0,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

For the estimate of
M_{84}
, applying Hölder’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle M_{84}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla_{v}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}\|f\log f\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\|u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{3}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\nabla_{v}\phi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}\|f\log f\|_{L^{\frac{3}{2}}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\times\left(\|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{3}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}+\|u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u\|_{L^{3}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}\right).

By Lemma
5.11
, it follows that

\displaystyle M_{84}\to 0,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

To sum up, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left(2+\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right)f^{\varepsilon,\delta}u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\cdot\nabla_{v}\phi dxdvds

\displaystyle\hskip 85.35826pt\rightarrow\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left(2+\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f\right)fu\cdot\nabla_{v}\phi dxdvds,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

The estimate of
M_{9}
:

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi-(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot\left(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)\right)f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-u^{\varepsilon,\delta})f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi dxdvds

\displaystyle+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot\left(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)\right)f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle M_{9-1}+M_{9-2}.

For
M_{9-1}
, it becomes

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-u^{\varepsilon,\delta})f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{4}{3}\pi\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}}+1\right)\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}|(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})|\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}_{v}^{3}}|v|^{3}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dv\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{4}{3}\pi\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}}+1\right)\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{4}(\Omega_{x}))}\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{4}(\Omega_{x}))}\left\||v|^{3}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t;L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{6}_{xv}))}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-u\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{4}(\Omega_{x}))}+\|u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{4}(\Omega_{x}))}+\|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{4}(\Omega_{x}))}\right)\rightarrow 0.

For
M_{9-2}
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi))dvdxds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\left|\left(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)\right)\cdot(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\right|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{4}{3}\pi\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}}+1\right)\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)-u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi||\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}|\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v|^{3}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dv\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}dxds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\left(\frac{4}{3}\pi\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}}+1\right)\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{4}(\Omega_{x}))}\times

\displaystyle\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{4}(\Omega_{x}))}\left\||v|^{3}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t;L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{6}_{xv}))}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\bigg(\|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u\psi)-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{4}(\Omega_{x}))}+\|u\psi-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u\psi)\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{4}(\Omega_{x}))}

\displaystyle+\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi-u\psi\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{4}(\Omega_{x}))}\bigg)\rightarrow 0,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

To sum up, we derive

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi-(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\cdot\left(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)\right)f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds\rightarrow 0,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

The estimate of
M_{10}
:
Similary with (
6
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|v|^{2}(1-\gamma_{\delta}(v))\phi dxdvds\rightarrow 0,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

The estimate of
M_{11}
:

\displaystyle M_{11}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}|v|^{3}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(1-\gamma_{\delta}(v))|\nabla_{v}\phi|dxdvds

\displaystyle+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}|vf^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|(1-\gamma_{\delta}(v))|\nabla_{v}\phi|dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ct\left\||v|^{3}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t;L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{6}_{xv}))}\left\|\nabla_{v}\phi\right\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}\left\|1-\gamma_{\delta}(v)\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega_{v})}

\displaystyle+\left\||v|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{1}((0,T)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}\left\|\nabla_{v}\phi\right\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v})}\left\|1-\gamma_{\delta}(v)\right\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega_{v})}.

Due to the boundedness of
\Omega_{v}
, as
\delta\rightarrow 0
, it follows that
\gamma_{\delta}(v)=1
in
\Omega_{v}
. It follows that

\displaystyle M_{11}\rightarrow 0,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

Finally, as
\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0
, one can get the energy inequality as follows

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega_{x}}\left(|u|^{2}\psi\right)(t,\cdot)dx+\int_{\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left(\left(\frac{1}{2}|v|^{2}f+f\log f\right)\phi\right)(t,\cdot)dxdv+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}|\nabla u|^{2}\psi dxds

(7.83)

\displaystyle+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\frac{|(u-v)f-\nabla_{v}f|^{2}}{f}\phi dxdvds\leq\frac{1}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}|u|^{2}(\partial_{t}\psi+\Delta_{x}\psi)dxds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}|u|^{2}u\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi dxds+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f\right)f(\partial_{t}\phi+\Delta_{v}\phi)dxdvds

\displaystyle+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f\right)fv\cdot\left(\nabla_{x}\phi-\nabla_{v}\phi\right)dxdvds+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}(P-\bar{P})u\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi dxds

\displaystyle-\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}fv\cdot u\phi dxdvds+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}fv\cdot u\psi dxdvds

\displaystyle+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\Omega_{v}}\left(2+\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f\right)fu\cdot\nabla_{v}\phi dxdv

8.
Verification of
vi)
in Definition
1.1
: the convergence of local energy inequality of the second type

Verification of local energy inequality in
vi)
of Definition
1.1
.
In the following, we will prove the convergence of the local energy inequality in (
4.3
). For the left-hand terms of it, the convergence is proved in Section 6, and we omit it. Let us write the right terms of the local energy inequality (
4.3
) as
N_{1},N_{2},\cdots,N_{8}
term by term. Since
M_{1}
,
M_{2}
,
M_{5}
and
M_{9}
are the same as
N_{1}
,
N_{2}
,
N_{3}
and
N_{4}
, we also omit it.

For
N_{5}
:

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|v|^{2}(1-\gamma_{\delta}(v))\psi dxdvds

\displaystyle=\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}|v|^{3}\frac{1-\gamma_{\delta}(v)}{|v|}\psi dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq\left\||v|^{3}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t;L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{6}_{xv}))}\left\|\frac{1-\gamma_{\delta}(v)}{|v|}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\left\|\psi\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{tx}}

\displaystyle\leq C\delta\left\||v|^{3}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t;L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{6}_{xv}))}\|\psi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}\rightarrow 0.

For
N_{6}
:

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}\bigg|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\big((\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta})f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f)f\big)dv\partial_{t}\psi\bigg|dxds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}\bigg|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}\big(f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\big)dv\partial_{t}\psi\bigg|dxds+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}\bigg|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\big(f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\log f\big)dv\partial_{t}\psi\bigg|dxds

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle N_{61}+N_{62}.

For
N_{61}
, due to Lemma
5.9
, there holds

\displaystyle N_{61}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\||v|^{2}\big(f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\big)\right\|_{L^{1}((0,t)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}\|\partial_{t}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}\rightarrow 0,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

For
N_{62}
, due to Lemma
5.15
, we have

\displaystyle N_{62}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\log f\right\|_{L^{1}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}\|\partial_{t}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}\rightarrow 0,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

In summary, we get

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\left(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right)f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\partial_{t}\psi dxdvds\rightarrow\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\left(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f\right)f\partial_{t}\psi dxdvds.

For
N_{7}
:

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}\bigg|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\big((\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta})f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f)f\big)vdv\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi\bigg|dxds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}\bigg|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\frac{|v|^{3}}{2}\big(f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\big)dv\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi\bigg|dxds

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}\bigg|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\big(f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\log f\big)vdv\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi\bigg|dxds

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle N_{71}+N_{72}.

For
N_{71}
, due to Lemma
5.9
, we have

\displaystyle N_{71}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\||v|^{3}\big(f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\big)\right\|_{L^{1}((0,t)\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}\|\nabla_{x}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}\rightarrow 0,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

For
N_{72}
, due to Lemma
5.16
, we have

\displaystyle N_{72}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\||v|\left|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}-f\log f\right|\right\|_{L^{1}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})}\|\nabla_{x}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}\rightarrow 0,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

To sum up, we get

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\left(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right)f^{\varepsilon,\delta}v\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi dxdvds

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt\rightarrow\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\left(\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}+\log f\right)fv\cdot\nabla_{x}\psi dxdvds,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

For
N_{8}
:

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}v\cdot\left(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)\right)-f^{\varepsilon,\delta}v\cdot\left(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right)\psi dxdvds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}v\cdot\left(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right)\psi dxdvds

\displaystyle+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}v\cdot\left(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)-u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi\right)dxdvds

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle N_{8-1}+N_{8-2}.

For
N_{8-1}
, it becomes

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}v\cdot\left(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right)\psi dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|\theta\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-u^{\varepsilon,\delta}||v|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{4}{3}\pi\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}}+1\right)\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-u^{\varepsilon,\delta})\psi\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dv\right)^{\frac{4}{5}}dxds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ct^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{4}{3}\pi\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}}+1\right)\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{5}(\Omega_{x}))}\left\||v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t;L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{6}_{xv}))}^{\frac{4}{5}}\|\psi\|_{L^{\infty}((0,t)\times\Omega_{x})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(t)\bigg(\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}-u\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{5}(\Omega_{x}))}+\|u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{5}(\Omega_{x}))}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{5}(\Omega_{x}))}\bigg)\rightarrow 0,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

For
N_{8-2}
, it shows that

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}v\cdot(\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)-u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)dvdxds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|\theta\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)-u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi||v|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dxdvds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{4}{3}\pi\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}}+1\right)\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)-u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi|\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}dv\right)^{\frac{4}{5}}dxds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Ct^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{4}{3}\pi\|f^{\varepsilon,\delta}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}}+1\right)\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{5}(\Omega_{x}))}\left\||v|^{2}f^{\varepsilon,\delta}\right\|_{L^{\infty}(0,t;L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{6}_{xv}))}^{\frac{4}{5}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(t)\bigg(\|\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u\psi)-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi)\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{5}(\Omega_{x}))}+\|u\psi-\theta_{\varepsilon}\ast(u\psi)\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{5}(\Omega_{x}))}

\displaystyle+\|u^{\varepsilon,\delta}\psi-u\psi\|_{L^{2}(0,t;L^{5}(\Omega_{x}))}\bigg)\rightarrow 0,~\text{as}~\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0.

Finally, as
\varepsilon,\delta\rightarrow 0
, we get the energy inequality as

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega_{x}}\left(|u|^{2}\psi\right)(t,\cdot)dx+\int_{\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\left(\left(\frac{1}{2}|v|^{2}f+f\log f\right)\psi\right)(t,\cdot)dxdv+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}|\nabla u|^{2}\psi dxds

\displaystyle+\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\frac{|(u-v)f-\nabla_{v}f|^{2}}{f}\psi dxdvds\leq\frac{1}{2}\int_{(0,t)\times\Omega_{x}}|u|^{2}(\partial_{t}\psi+\Delta_{x}\psi)dxds
